<div align="left"><table class="RegBitDef"><col width="73.29px"/><col width="540.88px"/><tr height="22.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 31:7</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL">Reserved, must be kept at reset value.</p></td></tr><tr height="88.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 6</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CLFSDET</span>: Clear late frame synchronization detection flag. </p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is write only.</p><p class="TableIndent">Programming this bit to 1 clears the LFSDET flag in the SAI_xSR register.</p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is not used in AC’97<span data-conditional="_sai1_spdif">or SPDIF </span>mode</p><p class="TableIndent">Reading this bit always returns the value 0. </p></td></tr><tr height="88.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 5</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CAFSDET</span>: Clear anticipated frame synchronization detection flag. </p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is write only.</p><p class="TableIndent">Programming this bit to 1 clears the AFSDET flag in the SAI_xSR register.</p><p class="TableIndent">It is not used in AC’97<span data-conditional="_sai1_spdif">or SPDIF </span>mode.</p><p class="TableIndent">Reading this bit always returns the value 0. </p></td></tr><tr height="88.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 4</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CCNRDY</span>: Clear Codec not ready flag. </p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is write only.</p><p class="TableIndent">Programming this bit to 1 clears the CNRDY flag in the SAI_xSR register.</p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is used only when the AC’97 audio protocol is selected in the SAI_xCR1 register.</p><p class="TableIndent">Reading this bit always returns the value 0. </p></td></tr><tr height="22.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 3</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL">Reserved, must be kept at reset value.</p></td></tr><tr height="102.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 2</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CWCKCFG</span>: Clear wrong clock configuration flag. </p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is write only.</p><p class="TableIndent">Programming this bit to 1 clears the WCKCFG flag in the SAI_xSR register.</p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is used only when the audio block is set as master (MODE[1] = 0) and <span data-conditional="_sai1_ckgen1,_sai1_ckgen3">NODIV</span> = 0 in the SAI_xCR1 register.</p><p class="TableIndent">Reading this bit always returns the value 0. </p></td></tr><tr height="72.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 1</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CMUTEDET</span>: Mute detection flag. </p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is write only.</p><p class="TableIndent">Programming this bit to 1 clears the MUTEDET flag in the SAI_xSR register.</p><p class="TableIndent">Reading this bit always returns the value 0. </p></td></tr><tr height="72.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 0</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">COVRUDR</span>: Clear overrun / underrun.</p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is write only.</p><p class="TableIndent">Programming this bit to 1 clears the OVRUDR flag in the SAI_xSR register.</p><p class="TableIndent">Reading this bit always returns the value 0.</p></td></tr></table></div>


### 43.6.16 SAI data register (SAI\_BDR)

Address offset: 0x40

Reset value: 0x0000 0000

<div align="left" data-conditional="_sai1_pdm"><table class="RegBitDef"><col width="73.29px"/><col width="540.88px"/><tr height="22.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 31:16</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL">Reserved, must be kept at reset value.</p></td></tr><tr height="22.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 15:<span data-conditional="_sai1_pdm,_sai1_pdm_4cklines">12</span></p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL">Reserved, must be kept at reset value.</p></td></tr><tr data-conditional="_sai1_pdm,_sai1_pdm_4cklines" height="121.33px"><td data-conditional="_sai1_pdm,_sai1_pdm_4cklines"><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 11</p></td><td data-conditional="_sai1_pdm,_sai1_pdm_4cklines"><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CKEN4</span>: Clock enable of bitstream clock number 4</p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is set and cleared by software. </p><p class="TableIndent">0: SAI_CK4 clock disabled</p><p class="TableIndent">1: SAI_CK4 clock enabled</p><p class="RegTNote">Note:<span style="padding-right:7pt ;">​</span>It is not recommended to configure this bit when PDMEN = 1.</p><p class="RegTNote_">SAI_CK4 might not be available for all SAI instances. Refer to <span style="font-style:normal;"><a href="#XREF_94026__64_3_SAI_implementation20" style="font-family : Arial ; font-weight:normal;font-style:Italic;font-size : 9.00pt;font-kerning:normal;font-stretch:normal;color:#0000ff;background-color:transparent;letter-spacing : 0.00em;">Section 43.3: SAI implementation</a></span> for details.</p></td></tr><tr data-conditional="_sai1_pdm,_sai1_pdm_4cklines" height="121.33px"><td data-conditional="_sai1_pdm,_sai1_pdm_4cklines"><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 10</p></td><td data-conditional="_sai1_pdm,_sai1_pdm_4cklines"><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CKEN3</span>: Clock enable of bitstream clock number 3</p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is set and cleared by software. </p><p class="TableIndent">0: SAI_CK3 clock disabled</p><p class="TableIndent">1: SAI_CK3 clock enabled</p><p class="RegTNote">Note:<span style="padding-right:7pt ;">​</span>It is not recommended to configure this bit when PDMEN = 1.</p><p class="RegTNote_">SAI_CK3 might not be available for all SAI instances. Refer to <span style="font-style:normal;"><a href="#XREF_94026__64_3_SAI_implementation20" style="font-family : Arial ; font-weight:normal;font-style:Italic;font-size : 9.00pt;font-kerning:normal;font-stretch:normal;color:#0000ff;background-color:transparent;letter-spacing : 0.00em;">Section 43.3: SAI implementation</a></span> for details.</p></td></tr><tr height="121.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 9</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CKEN2</span>: Clock enable of bitstream clock number 2</p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is set and cleared by software. </p><p class="TableIndent">0: SAI_CK2 clock disabled</p><p class="TableIndent">1: SAI_CK2 clock enabled</p><p class="RegTNote">Note:<span style="padding-right:7pt ;">​</span>It is not recommended to configure this bit when PDMEN = 1.</p><p class="RegTNote_">SAI_CK2 might not be available for all SAI instances. Refer to <span style="font-style:normal;"><a href="#XREF_12343__1_3_SAI_implementation20" style="font-family : Arial ; font-weight:normal;font-style:Italic;font-size : 9.00pt;font-kerning:normal;font-stretch:normal;color:#0000ff;background-color:transparent;letter-spacing : 0.00em;">Section 43.3: SAI implementation</a></span> for details.</p></td></tr><tr height="121.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 8</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">CKEN1</span>: Clock enable of bitstream clock number 1</p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is set and cleared by software. </p><p class="TableIndent">0: SAI_CK1 clock disabled</p><p class="TableIndent">1: SAI_CK1 clock enabled</p><p class="RegTNote">Note:<span style="padding-right:7pt ;">​</span>It is not recommended to configure this bit when PDMEN = 1.</p><p class="RegTNote_">SAI_CK1 might not be available for all SAI instances. Refer to <span style="font-style:normal;"><a href="#XREF_12343__1_3_SAI_implementation20" style="font-family : Arial ; font-weight:normal;font-style:Italic;font-size : 9.00pt;font-kerning:normal;font-stretch:normal;color:#0000ff;background-color:transparent;letter-spacing : 0.00em;">Section 43.3: SAI implementation</a></span> for details.</p></td></tr><tr height="22.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 7:6</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL">Reserved, must be kept at reset value.</p></td></tr><tr height="153.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 5:4</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">MICNBR[1:0]</span>: Number of microphones</p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is set and cleared by software. </p><p class="TableIndent">00: Configuration with 2 microphones</p><p class="TableIndent">01: Configuration with 4 microphones</p><p class="TableIndent">10: Configuration with 6 microphones</p><p class="TableIndent">11: Configuration with 8 microphones</p><p class="RegTNote">Note:<span style="padding-right:7pt ;">​</span>It is not recommended to configure this field when PDMEN = 1.*</p><p class="RegTNote_">The complete set of data lines might not be available for all SAI instances. Refer to <span style="font-style:normal;"><a href="#XREF_12343__1_3_SAI_implementation20" style="font-family : Arial ; font-weight:normal;font-style:Italic;font-size : 9.00pt;font-kerning:normal;font-stretch:normal;color:#0000ff;background-color:transparent;letter-spacing : 0.00em;">Section 43.3: SAI implementation</a></span> for details.</p></td></tr><tr height="22.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 3:1</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL">Reserved, must be kept at reset value.</p></td></tr><tr height="102.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 0</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">PDMEN</span>: PDM enable </p><p class="TableIndent">This bit is set and cleared by software. This bit enables to control the state of the PDM interface block.</p><p class="TableIndent">Make sure that the SAI in already operating in TDM master mode before enabling the PDM interface. </p><p class="TableIndent">0: PDM interface disabled</p><p class="TableIndent">1: PDM interface enabled</p></td></tr></table></div>
